Current density gate (2026-10-07 audit; overrides legacy-target admission):
   Legacy regularize_energy caps finite U at1e20, then applies a log tail above
   1e8. log_prob uses this altered energy, rather than clipping only a gradient.
   The positive-bond chart has unbounded support, derivative tending to1, and
   the Cartesian IC measure includes b^2 db. Along a terminal-bond extension,
   capped exp(-U_reg) times b^2 is not integrable. It is not an admissible
   physical Boltzmann target. Existing runs/metrics are retained as diagnostics;
   their reported physical-energy field actually means regularized Cartesian U.
   This density defect alone does not explain the finite-run quality failures.
   Do not use a legacy-target geometry result to unlock solver/noise/reference.

   New explicit energy_mode=physical keeps raw OpenMM U/(R*T) and forces in the
   log density, with FAB plus chart Jacobians. Terminal gradient norm clipping
   remains a separate training setting. Preserve the historical regularized
   default and immutable old jobs; pin energy_definition in banks/checkpoints.
   Old energy banks/checkpoints cannot be used under the physical definition.
   Physical preflight re-evaluates A/B energies on exactly the old coordinates,
   retaining transform, offsets, frame selections, quality thresholds and test
   closure. Check low-energy agreement, raw collision tails above the old cap,
   force/native finite differences, then one shared B4/N4 train/eval/checkpoint.
   Fresh source and output: physical_v1. Gate admits execution only. After this
   gate and legacy matched-budget diagnosis, use the smallest physical-density
   source/clip comparisons needed, then matched cuts at frozen nuisance settings.

1. Geometry stage (implemented; first remote submission)
   Freeze FAB transform and chart statistics on val.h5 frames 0+100*k, k<4096.
   Only phi and psi chart centers change. The other 58 coordinates stay fixed.
   Three cuts: current; smoothed calibration-histogram minimum; maximum.
   The maximum is a diagnostic control and is never selected for production.
   A fixed base-chart Gaussian is mapped to each chart. Its exact transformed
   source density/score are used; all charts start from the same physical law.
   Kernels/bridges remain Gaussian in y. No wrapping of a simulated y path.
   The cut cannot be crossed via finite y: its two sides correspond to +/-infinity.
   Physical cut selection is based on calibration only, never test/validation.

   Baseline: shared GammaASBSModule, legacy VE (0.001,0.5), ExponentialIntegrator,
   N=200, CE/full BC, score units, physical-time input, source base scale=1,
   original 8x256 FourierMLP, LR1e-4, epsilon_TR=1, fresh frozen-policy buffers.
   Pilot: 3 cuts x seed0. Confirmation: all 3 cuts x seeds1,2, same budget.
   Each: 2 outer loops x 20 FC/20 BC epochs x 2 optimizer steps, batch64,
   fresh buffers128/epoch, dual [32,8], raw final samples1024, Slurm limit2h.
   OpenMM cap50000 configurations/run; log setup/train/online/final separately.
   Preflight: 3 cuts, full architecture, B4/N4, one FC and one BC step; real
   energy/force, shared physical-source agreement, Jacobian and checkpoint.

2. Frozen validation banks and geometry decision
   Bank A: val frames 500000+50*k, k<4096; bank B: 750000+50*k, k<4096.
   These ranges and calibration are disjoint. Temporal correlation remains;
   neither bank nor A/B subsample floors assert MD reference convergence.
   Fixed physical metrics: 32x32 periodic phi/psi histogram TVD, marginal JS,
   4x4 coarse-basin occupancy/TVD/missing mass, physical energy W2 (kT),
   branch-cut visits, native tails, rejected steps, achieved finite-kernel KL.
   No raw samples are chirality/energy/quality filtered or silently discarded.
   Native energy is chart dependent and cannot select a chart across candidates.
   A is the selection bank. B is reported after selection; final test.h5 stays closed.
   Geometry pilot gate (not convergence): all 3 seeds pass each A threshold
   max(minimum, 3*A/B-floor-q95): TVD minimum0.30, marginal JS0.05,
   physical-energy-W2 8, coarse-basin-TVD0.15, missing mass0.10.
   Inspect paired seed differences and near-cut undercoverage, not only a winner.
   Do not eliminate candidates from the first short seed solely on occupancy.
   If both ordinary cuts fail, record geometry_not_admitted, diagnose before
   reference tuning. A terminal-quality failure is not a software bug or a reason
   to silently repeat the same job. Retain every failed/not_evaluated row.
   When an ordinary cut is admissible, choose the lower mean A TVD among those
   candidates, then disclose every B metric and cut-sensitivity limitation.

3. Later stages (authorized; prepare after the geometry evidence is reviewed)
   Freeze chart, physically matched source, transform, banks and capacity.
   Solver: Brownian geometric schedule with fixed integrated variance0.249999,
   N=200, physical time and common logvar loss: Euler / legacy / diffusion-time
   Euler. Compare only solver here; density implementation stays shared/corrected.
   Noise: winner solver, geometric / constant / cosine, fixed total variance/grid.
   Reference: winner Brownian vs scalar OU, same loss and matched raw noise budget;
   disclose OU transition Phi,K,Q and endpoint variance. VP is excluded when the
   chosen mapped source is non-Gaussian: the current VP stationary-source contract
   must not be bypassed. If current chart is selected, its base source is Gaussian
   and the existing VP guard can be used with matched stationary scale.
   Loss: best ordinary Brownian, logvar vs path KL; state gradients and actual
   kernel costs required. Do not mix this comparison with reference selection.
   Per stage at most3 initial candidates/seed0; retain top2 and add seeds1,2.
   Tune N={100,200,400}, variance={0.5,1,2} times baseline, epsilon_TR and LR
   sequentially around the winner; no Cartesian product. Equal optimizer and
   OpenMM-configuration budgets; wall time/hardware are additional provenance.
   Final frozen seeds/bank from separate test.h5; no tuning on final data.
   If cut dependence prevents reliable exploration, implement periodic kernels
   with their correct density/bridge/BC contracts before claiming a final sampler.

   Dedicated immutable source snapshot; preserve local dirty tree/remote checkouts.
   No new Git worktree. Existing tr_asbs worktrees on z3=3 at initial inspection.
   sbatch on z3 gpu/1 GPU/4 CPU/16G, fixed Reference OpenMM backend, hardware
   recorded without requiring a GPU model. Existing bad CUDA node excluded.
   Monitor invokes one scheduler/accounting/log/receipt-SHA check, never sleeps
   waiting for completion. Preflight must pass before geometry submissions.
   Ledger pins job ID, run/log paths, interpreter, source and plan SHA.
   10-minute heartbeat; after 2 consecutive pending/running checks use30 minutes.
   Failures: inspect logs first; simple implementation bugs get a versioned
   snapshot/output retry, preserving original jobs and artifacts. No duplicates.
   Completion/artifact verification and scientific admission are distinct states.
   Read shared reports/experiment-board.md before appending any update; fetch
   latest board changes if it later becomes shared through another Git branch.
